### CRM adapter Path A — chapter_internal adapter shipped for adsforgood_prod (July 28, 2026 morning)
- **Why it exists:** operator wanted identity-prompt captures on ads4good.com to land in a queryable Supabase table for follow-up outreach. Chapter's privacy contract stops raw email from persisting for external clients — but for agency-internal captures (adsforgood_prod tenant on ads4good.com), operator is the data owner + wants the raw list. Broader design supports future clients wanting Klaviyo / Mailchimp / HubSpot / Salesforce sync via the same adapter dispatch.
- **Schema (migration `add_crm_provider_to_clients`):** two new columns on `chapter_config.clients` — `crm_provider text` (null / `chapter_internal` / `klaviyo` / `mailchimp` / `hubspot` / `salesforce` / `custom`) + `crm_credentials_jsonb jsonb`. Null default preserves the privacy contract for every existing tenant except the one we opted in. Enabled `crm_provider='chapter_internal'` for `adsforgood_prod` only.
- **Adapter library at `src/app/lib/crm-adapter/`** — mirrors the ESP adapter pattern from MI v2 Phase 3. `types.ts` (`CrmProvider` union + `LeadInput` + `LeadResult` + `CrmAdapter` interface with `upsertLead(input)`). `selector.ts` picks the adapter based on `chapter_config.clients.crm_provider`; 5-min in-memory cache. 5 provider implementations: `internal.ts` (live), `klaviyo.ts` / `mailchimp.ts` / `hubspot.ts` / `salesforce.ts` (documented stubs returning `action:'not_implemented'` — each file carries the API shape + credentials JSON spec in comments so future implementation is "fill in the API call"). `custom.ts` shell inline in selector.
- **Internal adapter upsert semantic:** SELECT id from `crm.prospects` by `.ilike('email', email)` (uses the existing `prospects_email_idx` on lower(email)). If found → UPDATE: merge tags (append `'identity_prompt'` + `'prompt:<slug>'` if not present), append submission record to `metadata.submissions[]` (capped at 50 for storage), update `last_touch_at`, upgrade `phone_number` and `contact_name` if we now have them and prior row didn't. If not found → INSERT: synthesize `business_name` from email domain (`user@company.com` → `company.com`), generate `prospect_key` from email-local + random hex, `stage='new'`, `source='inbound'` (matches existing vocabulary — the 9-value convention doesn't include an `identity_prompt` value; tags carry the fine-grained origin), `consent_mode='opt_in'` (default; per-submission consent detail lives in the metadata submission record), full metadata block with `origin`, `first_captured_via`, `first_captured_page_url`, `first_captured_client_key`, and the initial `submissions` array. Returns `{action: 'created' | 'updated' | 'skipped' | 'error', prospect_id?, error?}`.
- **Wiring in `/api/chapter/identity-prompt-email`:** after `resend.emails.send` succeeds, `void (async () => { const adapter = await selectCrmAdapter(clientKey); if (adapter) await adapter.upsertLead({email: recipient, prompt_slug: slug, page_url: referer, ip_country: ...}) })()`. Fire-and-forget — never blocks the visitor response. Failures log + swallow. No CRM behavior for tenants without `crm_provider` set (null adapter → no-op).
- **Coverage today (Email Exchange with email post-submit only):** the only path where raw email exists server-side today is `/api/chapter/identity-prompt-email`, which Chapter needs raw email for to call Resend. `exit_intent_recovery` on adsforgood_prod uses this path. First submission after deploy lands in `crm.prospects`.
- **Accepted coverage gaps (fixable later):**
  1. **Phone-only Email Exchange (`input_mode='phone'`)** — pixel hashes phone client-side and sends `phone_sha256:X` to `/api/identify`. Raw phone never reaches server. Would need Path B or a per-prompt "keep raw" pixel flag to capture.
  2. **Custom Form identity-marked email/phone fields (`for_identity=true`, the default)** — same hashing pattern. Non-identity fields (name, company, role, notes) DO reach `/api/chapter/prompt-response` raw; extending that endpoint to also call the CRM adapter is the natural follow-up when we build a Custom Form that uses those fields.
  3. **External CRM adapters** — stubs return `action:'not_implemented'` cleanly. When first client asks for Klaviyo / Mailchimp / HubSpot / Salesforce, ~50 lines each per the documented API shapes.
- **Path B — browser piggyback callback (designed, not shipped).** `window.ChapterPixel.onPromptSubmit(cb)` hook via the existing queue pattern — client's 3rd party JS (Klaviyo `klaviyo.identify` / HubSpot `_hsq.push(['identify'])` / etc.) registers a callback that receives raw payload from the browser BEFORE Chapter's own hashing/network. Chapter never sees raw. ~40 lines in pixel.js. Ships when a client explicitly wants "don't touch our data flow" — most clients will prefer Path A because they want Chapter to manage the sync.
- **Commit:** `445b496`.

### Internal builder redesign + consent/ack port from self-serve (July 27, 2026)
- **Operator UX wins across the entire `/internal/*` builder surface.** User feedback: "the self-serve dash is so much easier to use, how good it looks (layout + color), and the prompt/submission preview" — asked for that same look on the operator surface while keeping all operator-only features. Delivered in two ships: full builder redesign (`c0411b1`) + consent/ack feature port with preview wiring (`256ba90` + `80a1938`).
- **Shared design primitives at `src/app/lib/ui/builder-primitives.tsx`** (325 lines) — new file with color tokens (`INK #1F2D43` / `MUTED #5C6B82` / `FAINT #8A98AD` / `ORANGE #E36410` / `LINE #E5E0D4` / `PANEL` / `SUBTLE` / `GREEN` / `DANGER`) + reusable components (`Section` wrapper, `NumRow`, `BackLink`, `PillButton`, `PrimaryButton`, `SecondaryButton`, `ErrorBanner`, `PageHeader`) + shared style objects (`inp`, `inpMono`, `miniBtn`). Self-serve `PromptEditor.tsx` + `LinkEditor.tsx` updated to import from here (deleted local duplicates). Establishes the single design-language source of truth both surfaces share.
- **`/internal/identity-prompts/[clientKey]/PromptForm.tsx`** (1,088 → 860 lines): full rewrite. Two-column layout (form left, sticky live preview right). 3-col preset picker with locked-once-created behavior (all 6 presets — Email Exchange / Custom Form / Custom Notification / Make an Offer / Phone Call / Remind Me placeholder). `PromptPreview` from self-serve wired for the 4 supported presets; `PreviewPlaceholder` card for the 3 that can't preview (Make an Offer needs runtime product+threshold resolution, Remind Me is Phase 6, multi-page Custom Form isn't in self-serve's preview scope). All 6 preset builders (CustomFormBuilder / MultiPageBuilder / RecoveryBuilder / NotificationBuilder / PhoneCallBuilder / MakeAnOfferBuilder) kept wired unchanged. All 5 post-submit actions preserved (message / button / redirect / email / email_message). Email template fields preserved.
- **List page + edit chrome + row actions** on identity-prompts + admin sub-routes (templates / offers / offer-thresholds / responses) all restyled to match self-serve card-row layout + PillButton row actions (Edit / Turn off/on / Delete).
- **`/internal/redirect-rules/[clientKey]/RuleForm.tsx`** — same two-column treatment: form left, sticky URL tester + live resolved-URL preview + token explainer right. All 17 condition types preserved in ConditionBuilder. 6 destination presets + full token dictionary + placeholder substitution preserved.
- **All layouts + list pages** on redirect-rules restyled to match. Zero server-action / DB / preset business logic changes — purely UI restyle.
- **Follow-up port (`256ba90` + `80a1938`)**: **Consent controls** (Off / Checkbox / Ask Yes-No pills) added to Email Exchange + Custom Form — matches self-serve's compliance-oriented pattern with EU/SMS pre-check warning + explainer note for the Yes-No mode ("No" leads still captured but flagged declined). **Acknowledgement message** field added to Custom Notification when CTA type is yes_no or button. Both write to columns that already existed from the self-serve build (`consent_jsonb` + `submit_actions_jsonb.ack_message`) — no schema migration needed. **Preview wiring fix (`80a1938`):** initial ship had `consentMode: "off"` and `notifAck: ""` hardcoded in the `previewData` object → preview never rendered the new controls even when configured; fix threads the state through. `shapePayload` in `_actions.ts` gates the consent write on preset_type so notification/phone_call/make_an_offer/remind_me never persist a stray consent element.
- **Nothing dropped, nothing stubbed** beyond the 3 unpreviewable presets (Make an Offer / Remind Me / multi-page Custom Form) which show a preset-specific placeholder card in the preview column with an honest reason. Compile clean throughout (both ships zero source errors).

### Fair-use clause + verified-journey pricing swap across 3 surfaces (July 27, 2026)
- **Business-model shift codified**: Chapter now bills on *verified customer journeys* (bot-classified sessions only) instead of raw journey counts (which included bots). Numbers look smaller for Growth + Pro tiers but at Chapter's ~60% bot ratio, **75K verified ≈ 187K raw and 150K verified ≈ 375K raw** — the ceilings are actually *more* generous. Chapter absorbs the bot cost. That's the pitch.
- **Terms page (`/terms-disclaimer`) — 3 new paragraphs in Section 3 (Self-serve SaaS):** (a) "Billable metric: verified customer journeys" — classifier version preserved in each monthly snapshot for audit; (b) "Tier ceilings & fair use" — Standard 25K · Growth 75K · Pro 150K with spike-absorption promise + 2-consecutive-month rule before any tier-upgrade conversation; (c) "Billing transparency" — points at the Billing page for raw vs verified breakdown. Copy tone-matched to the existing conversational-legal voice.
- **Lifecycle-attribution page (`/for-businesses/lifecycle-attribution`) — pricing card ceilings updated:** Standard `Up to 25K` → `Up to 25K verified customer journeys`, Growth `Up to 100K journeys` → `Up to 75K verified customer journeys`, Pro `Up to 200K journeys` → `Up to 150K verified customer journeys`. New helper line above the grid: "All tiers exclude bot & low-signal traffic from your billable journey count — you only pay for verified customer activity." with inline link to `/terms-disclaimer#saas`. Dollar amounts unchanged ($149 / $399 / $799).
- **Chapter subdomain (`chapter.ads4good.com`) pricing cards** — separate deployment (Lovable), can't touch from this repo. Prompt drafted for the sibling chat + operator applied same changes there for consistency.
- Impact: the tier ceilings in the `tier_journey_ceiling(p_tier)` DB helper (`standard=25K / growth=75K / pro=150K`, added June 30 Fix 1A) now match the customer-facing pricing surfaces. The customer contract, the marketing site, and the DB math all tell the same story: verified customer journeys, bot traffic excluded, spikes absorbed.
- Commit: `23b2388`.

### 5 log-surfaced bug fixes + preventive lockdowns (July 24, 2026)
- **Investigation trigger:** two docs came in from a parallel Claude Chat session flagging Postgres error-log patterns while investigating an unrelated `crm.interactions` alert. Verified all claims against actual DB + code state, corrected two misdiagnoses in the docs, shipped fixes.
- **Fix #1 — `crm.interactions` producer type vocabulary bug.** The nightly cron at `/api/internal/cron/crm-interactions-producer` was writing `type='inquiry_submitted'`. But `interactions_type_check` allows only nouns (`site_visit / gbp / yelp / inquiry / review / referral / other`). Every attempted mirror since June 19 (when the producer shipped) was silently rejected — 0 rows ever landed for inquiry mirroring. Fix: cron writes `'inquiry'`; partial dedup index rebuilt with matching WHERE predicate. Doc misdiagnosis: guessed the producer was `/api/inquiry` (wrong — that only writes `website_ops.website_inquiries`; the CRM producer is the nightly cron on a completely different code path). Doc's Resend-blocked hypothesis was also wrong — /api/inquiry Resend calls are non-blocking try/catch, unrelated. Impact today: 1 test prospect (Katoa's). Backfills automatically on the cron's next run. Commit: `904333f`.
- **Fix #2 — `identity_aliases` collision silent-drop (HIGH-value correctness fix).** Two overlapping unique indexes: `identity_aliases_from_unique` on `(client_key, from_identity_key)` (2-col) strictly subsumed `identity_aliases_unique` (3-col). All 7 app-side INSERT sites named the 3-col constraint on `ON CONFLICT`. When a real collision fired (same anon → different destination identity — shared devices, second email, gift purchases), the 2-col constraint threw, `ON CONFLICT` didn't match, outer try/catch swallowed → alias silently dropped forever. Real-scenario data loss — very low volume (Doc measured 2 in a window; 30-day pixel_events probe found 0 multi-email anons) but non-recoverable. Fix strategy (last-wins per operator decision): all 7 INSERTs updated to `ON CONFLICT (client_key, from_identity_key) DO UPDATE`. New `chapter_identity.identity_alias_conflicts` side table + `trg_log_alias_conflict` AFTER UPDATE trigger captures every `to_identity_key` change for observability. Dropped the redundant 3-col index (removed the exact naming-trap that caused the bug). Sites updated: `/api/purchase` Phases 1/2/3.5, `/api/identify`, `/api/alias`, `/api/conversion`, `lib/square/probabilistic-stitch`, `lib/redirect/identity-stitch`. Documented site-specific caveat on probabilistic-stitch: pure last-wins means a probabilistic match (confidence ≤80, `is_deterministic=false`) could now overwrite a prior deterministic (confidence=100) mapping — trigger will surface any real occurrence, and inline comment shows the confidence-guard SQL to add if it becomes a problem. **Deploy-transition regression note:** Vercel stale-warm-Lambdas kept serving old 3-col code for ~2 minutes during the rollover, spraying `there is no unique or exclusion constraint matching the ON CONFLICT specification` errors that landed in Postgres logs. Self-healed as instances cycled. 41 aliases wrote successfully within 20 min of deploy — verified end-to-end. Commit: `904333f`.
- **Fix #3 — `pixel_events.journey_id → journeys.id` FK: CASCADE → RESTRICT.** Purely preventive. No active bug. Table had ON DELETE CASCADE so if anyone ever prunes `chapter_journey.journeys` (currently ~1M rows — tempting), the pixel event history would silently vanish with it. RESTRICT forces any future deletion to be a two-step intentional decision. Commit: `904333f`.
- **Fix #4 — dead purchase-audit `pixel_events` INSERT (root cause of ~2.5 months of `null value in column journey_id` errors).** `/api/purchase` Phase 6 hardcoded `journey_id = null` in an "identify audit event" INSERT — but `pixel_events.journey_id` is NOT NULL, so every Shopify/Square purchase since Fix #26 Part 2 (May 13) threw the error and swallowed it in the outer try/catch. Zero rows ever landed (verified: 30-day probe found 413 total `event_name='identify'` events, **0** from `/api/purchase`). CLAUDE.md itself had flagged this as "latent NOT-NULL constraint footgun preserved as latent" back in May — this closes it. The identity-resolution audit trail this block was trying to create is fully captured elsewhere in the same handler (`identity_aliases.reason='purchase_identify_call'` + `identity_canon` upserts + `purchase_events.resolved_identity_key/identity_confidence/identity_reason` columns) — nothing downstream expected these rows. Fix: deleted the block + the unused `resolvedIdentityKey` variable. Verified 48h post-deploy: 51 purchases across 3 clients processed, zero `null value in column journey_id` errors in Postgres logs. Commit: `7db1db2`.
- **Fix #5 — dashboard scaffolding cleanup (root cause of `chapter_attribution.attribution_first_touch does not exist` errors every 2 sec while /demo was open).** `public.dashboard_snapshot_for_client` + `public.dashboard_snapshot_v1` + `chapter_attribution.attribution_linear` were Fix #10 (May 7) "scaffolding for the planned /chapter/* dashboard build" — kept intentionally at the time. That dashboard shipped in June (10/10 pages live) using dedicated per-tile RPCs in `dashboard-rpc.ts` — never touched the scaffolding. Meanwhile the scaffolding function referenced 5 attribution objects, 4 of which had subsequently been dropped elsewhere. `/demo/snapshot` polled the RPC every 2s while `/demo` (now `/internal/demo`) was open → every call threw `relation does not exist` on the first missing table. Silent errors since May 7 (~2.5 months of log noise, no visible product impact because the RPC's `dashboard_json` output was never actually rendered by the demo page). Fix: dropped all 3 orphan objects + simplified `/demo/snapshot` to skip the dead RPC call entirely (`dashboard_json` kept as empty-defaults in the response shape for API-compat). Commit: `0620e31`.
- **/demo/dashboard deprecation (July 26).** Separate mock-EOS dashboard demo at `/demo/dashboard` was also publicly accessible on ads4good.com — same concern as the /demo → /internal/demo move but not moved in the July 20 ship. Operator confirmed the page isn't needed anymore. Deleted `/demo/dashboard/page.tsx` + the entire orphan dependency chain (`DashboardShell` + `DashboardParts` + `configs/eos` + `dashboard/types` + `dashboard/formatters`) — 733 lines gone. `dashboard/freshness.ts` kept (still used by real `/chapter/(authed)/layout.tsx` for the "Data as of…" ribbon). Commit: `cfebda6`.
- **No unique data loss on any fix.** Fix #1 backfills automatically. Fix #2 lost some alias edges in collisions (very small volume, non-recoverable, but visibility now via conflict log). Fix #3 preventive. Fix #4 audit rows never wrote, but same info was captured in 3 other tables (identity_aliases + identity_canon + purchase_events). Fix #5 was a read-only failure of a dead code path. Full breakdown in commit messages.

### Practice env, /demo → /internal/demo, /api/chapter/c rename, /contact modal auto-open (July 20, 2026)
- **`/internal/prompt-playground` shipped** — practice surface for MI v2 prompts against a rich DOM without touching real client sites. New `chapter_practice` tenant provisioned (per-client Postgres role + HMAC secret + CLIENT_ROLE_MAP entry + storefront_domain=ads4good.com) — isolated from `adsforgood_prod` so practice prompts can't fire on the public site pixel. Rich labeled DOM (nav / hero + CTA / 6-product grid with per-product data-attrs / signup + contact forms / book-now CTA / mock cart / footer). Sidebar shows all live prompts for chapter_practice with trigger info + hit/submit counters. Live event feed at bottom (reuses `/demo/snapshot`). "Reset frequency caps" button clears the localStorage/sessionStorage keys the pixel uses for `session`/`visitor` frequency throttling so operators can re-fire the same prompt during iteration. Workflow: author prompt at `/internal/identity-prompts/chapter_practice` → open playground → click matching element → see prompt fire end-to-end. Commit: `e96a83f`.
- **`/demo` → `/internal/demo` migration** (same commit). `/demo` was publicly accessible on ads4good.com. Moved to `/internal/demo` behind the CHAPTER_DASH_TOKEN middleware gate. Old `/demo` route becomes a server redirect via `next/navigation redirect()` so bookmarks + sales-demo screen-shares keep working (screen-share workflow is: operator hits `/internal/demo` in their authed browser, prospect sees the screen). `/demo/snapshot` API route stays at original path (shared by both `/internal/demo` and `/internal/prompt-playground`; changing its path would break both). ChapterLoader updated: skip on `/internal/*` (was only skipping `/chapter/*`) so the practice playground can manually inject its own pixel with `data-client-key="chapter_practice"` without the global adsforgood_prod loader pre-empting it.
- **`/api/chapter/collect` → `/api/chapter/c` rename** (commit `0a7fc94`). Defuses ad-blocker filter-list rules that pattern-match on the substring `collect` (GA endpoint fingerprint — EasyList / EasyPrivacy / uBlock all pattern on it). No evidence of active blocking today; preventive hardening for scale. New route at `/api/chapter/c/route.ts` is a 1-line re-export from `../collect/route.ts` so both paths dispatch to the same handler — same bot filter, same rate limit, same forward to `/api/pixel`. `getCollectUrl()` in `pixel.js/route.ts` fallback path swapped to `/c`. All 4 clients' pixels auto-migrate on next page load (pixel.js served with `Cache-Control: no-store`; no theme code change required). Old `/collect` alive as alias — remove after ~4 weeks of zero-traffic verification. Verified live: both paths return 400 `missing_client_key` on curl (handler ran on both).
- **Test 1 setup: `sctest.ads4good.com/sctest` endpoint** (commit `858308d`). Cookie durability test for the A-record + HttpOnly:false + Server-set + Max-Age=31536000 config Chapter's pixel API actually uses. sctest subdomain provisioned as A record → Vercel anycast (verified via `dig`, not CNAME cloak). Endpoint sets `sctest_id=<uuid>|<setAt_epoch_ms>` cookie on first visit; preserves existing value on subsequent visits so we can measure real cookie age across ITP's 7-day cap threshold. HTML page renders "Cookie still present — set X days ago" or "Fresh cookie set just now" so the operator can eyeball without DevTools. Day-3 (July 22-23) PASSED. Day-7 (July 27) PROVISIONAL PASS — cookie present at 7 days, no ITP reap. Day-8 confirmation pending morning of July 28.
- **`/contact` URL-param auto-open** (commit `e9eb2a4`). Chapter subdomain site (`chapter.ads4good.com`) has CTAs that link to `/contact?open=inquiry&service=X&source=Y` — main ads4good.com contact page now auto-opens the inquiry modal with pre-selected service + attribution source. `useSearchParams()` inside Suspense (App Router requirement); `useState` lazy-init freezes the URL params ONCE so the subsequent `router.replace()` URL-strip doesn't reset the modal defaults on rerender. `InquiryLauncher` extended with `autoOpen?: boolean` prop; `useEffect` fires modal open once on mount when true. Manual click behavior on the same button unchanged when prop absent.

### Self-serve SaaS — Phases 3 + 4a SHIPPED + review-session UX pass (July 21, 2026)
- **Phase 3 — Smart Prompts self-serve (MVP).** Client-facing tool surface at `/chapter/<key>/prompts/*` for tools-only tenants. **Security model (the crux):** all reads/writes go through `withSelfServeClient` (shared `chapter_selfserve` NOBYPASSRLS role + `identity_prompts_client_isolation` RLS policy — enforces `client_key` on both USING and WITH CHECK), with `client_key` derived from the SESSION (`getCurrentChapterUser().client_key`), never form input. Verified at DB (role has CRUD grants; chapter_app is a member so runtime SET ROLE works). jsonb bound via `tx.json()`. Files under `src/app/chapter/(authed)/prompts/`: `types.ts`, `_actions.ts` (create/update/toggle/delete/list/get + getActivationStatus + setStorefrontDomain + listResponses), `PromptsClient.tsx` (list), `PromptEditor.tsx` (all 4 presets: Email Exchange uses the dedicated v1.5 columns; Custom Form / Custom Notification / Phone Call **reuse the operator builders directly** — `import CustomFormBuilder/NotificationBuilder/PhoneCallBuilder from "@/app/internal/identity-prompts/[clientKey]/…"`, self-contained value/onChange components, output mapped onto content_blocks_jsonb / form_fields_jsonb / container_jsonb / submit_actions_jsonb), `install/` (**Install & Activate** — capture `storefront_domain` for CORS via service_role, copy-paste pixel snippet on the `www.ads4good.com` origin, live "Connected/waiting" check against `chapter_ingest.pixel_events`), `responses/page.tsx` (show→submit summary + captured `chapter_engagement.prompt_responses`). Scope excludes email-sending post-submit (needs per-tenant ESP) + Make an Offer/Remind Me.
- **Phase 4a — Smart Links self-serve.** `/chapter/<key>/links/*`. A "link" = one slug + a required default destination + an ordered list of smart rules (context conditions → destination). Saved by **replace-all** of that slug's `chapter_config.redirect_rules` rows in ONE `withSelfServeClient` tx (smart rules at ascending priority, default catch-all last). RLS `redirect_rules_client_isolation` (verified grants). The public `/r/<key>/<slug>` route reads via service_role → self-serve links go live immediately, no infra. `SmartLinkConditions.tsx` is the operator ConditionBuilder **forked + trimmed to 9 context-only types** (device/os/country/region/day/hour/date-range/query-param/referrer — behavioral + A/B excluded as the upsell; emits identical `condition_jsonb`). Files: `types.ts`, `_actions.ts` (listLinks/getLink/saveLink/deleteLink/setLinkEnabled, gated on `smart_links` entitlement), `LinksClient.tsx`, `LinkEditor.tsx`. Link URL today = `www.ads4good.com/r/<key>/<slug>` (branded domain = Phase 4b).
- **Edge build gotcha (fixed):** `middleware.ts` imports `chapter-user.ts` and runs on the Edge runtime — `import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto"` broke the Vercel build (local Turbopack only warned). Fixed with Web Crypto (`globalThis.crypto.getRandomValues`). Memory: `feedback_middleware_edge_no_node_builtins`.
- **Review-session UX pass (tab-by-tab on k_man):** Home tiles link into the live tools (dropped "coming soon") with a 3-state smart CTA (prompts: install → create → manage); getting-started checklist is dynamic (ticks off from live DB state) + sectioned General / Smart Prompts / Smart Links. Install Step-1 shows currently-saved domain + "one website per workspace, saving overrides" note. Prompt editor widened + **left-justified two-column layout with a live preview** (`PromptPreview.tsx` — browser mock rendering all 4 presets live). **Sticky-scroll fix:** `.chapter-app` + `.main` `overflow-x: hidden` → `clip` in `chapter.css` (hidden makes them scroll containers and traps `position: sticky`; clip keeps horizontal-bleed protection without the scroll-container side effect) — GLOBAL to `/chapter/*`.
- **Pixel-powered CSS element picker (SHIPPED):** click-element triggers now have a "Pick from my site →" button → opens `https://<storefront_domain>/#__chapter_pick` → the installed pixel detects the flag (or persisted `sessionStorage.__chapter_pick`), **skips firing prompts**, and injects `/api/chapter/picker.js` (hover-highlight + click-select + proven selector-gen reused from `/api/internal/picker.js`); "Use this element" → `window.opener.postMessage({type:"chapter_pick_selector",selector})` → editor validates `e.source === opened tab` and fills the field. Persists across page navigation via sessionStorage; requires the pixel to be installed on the site to appear. Files: `api/chapter/picker.js/route.ts` (edge; DON'T combine `runtime:"edge"` with `dynamic:"force-static"` or it warns), pixel injection in `api/chapter/pixel.js/route.ts`, button+listener in `PromptEditor.tsx` (`storefront_domain` passed from new/edit pages; `getClientEntitlement` now returns `storefront_domain`).

### Self-serve SaaS — Phases 0–2 SHIPPED (Smart Prompts + Smart Links) (July 21, 2026)
- **Goal:** turn Smart Prompts (Moment Identity) + Smart Links (Chapter Links) into self-serve SaaS on the EXISTING repo/app. Users sign up + activate on their own. The `/chapter/[client_key]/*` surface serves BOTH self-serve signups AND existing Chapter clients self-managing. `/internal/*` operator admin is UNTOUCHED. Full plan + phase status in memory `project_self_serve_saas_build`. MVP = Phases 0–3; this session shipped 0–2 (the shell + auth). Phase 3 (Smart Prompts builder) is next.
- **Locked model:** ONE entitlement gate drives the whole surface — `tools_enabled text[]` on `chapter_config.clients`. Includes `chapter` → full analytics dashboard (operators + existing clients, unchanged). Excludes it → stripped self-serve shell. They compose: a full client who also self-manages prompts can have `{chapter, smart_prompts}`.
- **Phase 0 (foundation, PROD DB migrations `selfserve_phase0a/0b`):**
  - 0A — entitlement columns on `chapter_config.clients`: `plan`, `tools_enabled text[] DEFAULT '{chapter}'`, `billing_status DEFAULT 'operator'`, `trial_ends_at`, `self_serve boolean DEFAULT false`, `stripe_customer_id`, `stripe_subscription_id`. Existing 5 clients backfilled (self_serve=false, tools={chapter}).
  - 0B — shared role **`chapter_selfserve`** (NOLOGIN NOINHERIT NOBYPASSRLS, granted to `chapter_app`) + `<table>_client_isolation` RLS policy (`client_key = current_setting('app.client_key', true)`) on 8 tenant-tool tables (chapter_config.{identity_prompts, redirect_rules, redirect_ab_experiments, offer_thresholds, email_templates}, chapter_engagement.{prompt_responses, offers, subscriptions}). Code helper `withSelfServeClient(clientKey, fn)` in [per-client.ts](src/app/lib/db/per-client.ts) (SET LOCAL ROLE chapter_selfserve + set app.client_key). **Self-serve writes MUST go through this, never service_role; client_key always from session, never client input.** Inert until Phase 3 uses it.
  - 0C — [cors.ts](src/app/lib/auth/cors.ts) made DB-driven + fail-safe (static seed checked first so existing clients always pass; non-seed origins consult a 5-min cache of `chapter_config.clients` storefront_domain+redirect_host; DB failure degrades to seed). The dashboard `CLIENTS`-mock replacement was done additively in Phase 2 (below).
- **Phase 1 (open signup + auto-provision):** `/chapter/signup` (client component; Name/Phone/Email/Company + live client_key preview) → `POST /api/chapter-auth/signup` (open magic link, no allowlist; stages `chapter_config.pending_signups`; existing accounts get a plain login link) → callback `?signup=1` → `provisionSelfServeTenant()` → `/chapter/<key>/welcome`. Migration `selfserve_phase1_provisioning`: `users.full_name/phone`, `clients.business_name`, `pending_signups` table, function `chapter_config.provision_self_serve_tenant(...)` (slugifies company → client_key: single-word → `name_01/_02`, multi-word → slug then `_02`; inserts clients self_serve=true+trialing+trial_ends_at=+30d+tools={smart_prompts,smart_links} + client_secrets + client_employee users row). First real self-serve tenant: `k_man`.
- **Phase 2 (entitlement-driven surface):**
  - Entitlement resolver `getClientEntitlement(clientKey)` in [chapter-user.ts](src/app/lib/auth/chapter-user.ts) (reads clients row, 5-min cache, FAILS OPEN to full analytics on error / missing tools_enabled → `["chapter"]`) + `isToolsOnly(ent)` + `defaultLandingSlug(clientKey)`.
  - Threading (additive; the deferred-0C registry rewrite): `(authed)/layout.tsx` resolves entitlement + a synthesized `Client` (for tenants NOT in the operator `CLIENTS` mock) for `role==='client_employee'`, passes `entitlement` + `selfServeClient` to `ChapterProvider`. `ChapterContext` gains `entitlement: EntitlementInfo` (default = full analytics, so operators/staff/legacy unaffected) + client fallback uses `selfServeClient` when the mock misses. **Fixes the latent bug where a self-serve tenant (not in CLIENTS) would have rendered as `eos_fabrics`.**
  - `Sidebar.tsx` early-returns `<SelfServeSidebar>` when `!toolsEnabled.includes('chapter')`: Home / (Smart Prompts if enabled) / (Smart Links if enabled) / Account (Billing, Settings, Support→inbox). Tool bodies (Prompts, Responses, Install, Links, Domain) render disabled with a **"Soon" pill** (Phases 3–4). Static workspace label shows trial ("Free trial · Nd left").
  - New pages: `(authed)/home/page.tsx` (welcome + trial banner + per-tool tiles + getting-started checklist), `(authed)/settings/page.tsx` (read-only workspace details). `(authed)/billing/page.tsx` branches → `SelfServeBilling.tsx` (trial/plan card + "Manage plan — coming soon" Stripe placeholder) when `self_serve || isToolsOnly`; operator usage view unchanged.
  - Landing: self-serve client_employees land on `/home` (callback destination uses `defaultLandingSlug`; middleware redirects the bare `/chapter/<key>` root to the entitlement-appropriate landing — one cached lookup, only on that exact path). Welcome page gained an "Enter your workspace →" CTA.
- **Auth email flow fix (BOTH Supabase templates; confirmed working):** the default `{{ .ConfirmationURL }}` uses the IMPLICIT flow → session tokens land in the URL `#fragment`, unreadable by the server callback → `callback_failed`. FIX = rewrite the email link to `{{ .RedirectTo }}&token_hash={{ .TokenHash }}&type=<TYPE>`. **"Magic Link"** template (returning logins) → `type=magiclink`. **"Confirm signup"** template (new signups AND allowlisted users' first login, since signInWithOtp has `shouldCreateUser:true`) → `type=signup`. Both rewritten with afG logo (`https://www.ads4good.com/images/ads4Good_Logo_500x500.png`) + orange button + plain-URL fallback. **If a template resets to the default, login/signup breaks again.** Companion route change ([magic-link/route.ts](src/app/api/chapter-auth/magic-link/route.ts)): dropped the `next='/chapter'` default (was force-overriding the callback's role landing → dumping everyone on operator Observations); emailRedirectTo now always carries a base `?login=1` param so the email template's `&token_hash=` append is always a valid URL. Net: login lands self-serve on `/home`, full clients on `/overview`, staff on `/chapter`. Resend SMTP already configured for Supabase Auth (smtp.resend.com:465, verified ads4good.com domain).
- **Deferred to later phases:** Phase 3 (Smart Prompts client-facing builder via `withSelfServeClient`+RLS, install-pixel page, Responses view, show→submit analytics — un-disables Prompts nav); Phase 4 (Smart Links + branded-domain connect via Vercel Domains API); Phase 5 (Stripe Checkout + Customer Portal — pricing TBD); Phase 6 (wire marketing-page "Start free" CTAs). v1 preset scope for Smart Prompts = Email Exchange / Custom Form / Custom Notification / Phone Call (defer Make an Offer + Remind Me). Smart Links conditions = context-only (device/OS/geo/time/query/referrer/AB); behavioral conditions need pixel ingest (natural upsell to full Chapter).

The chip title carries the exact prior-resolved date. `weeksAgo` is computed client-side against `Date.now()`. Handles both object + array PostgREST responses (defensive against schema-cache oddities).

Bids $60+ auto-accept via shopify-mock returning `CHAPTER-XXXXXXXX` codes; $53-59 counter-offer; <$53 decline. Set `chapter_config.clients.platform = 'shopify'` for adsforgood_prod so the selector routes to shopifyMock (no creds → mock fallback). Demo now exercises all three offer-decision paths end-to-end.

Progress dots + Back button on. Showcases the Phase 2B branching feature that operators previously had to hand-author JSON to configure.

Widened the constraint to accept both. Migration: `offer_thresholds_align_target_type_with_code`.

Partial index on `(client_key, severity_override) WHERE severity_override IS NOT NULL` for the rare-but-scannable case. `severity_override_check` allows only the 3 severity values — `dismissed_at` already exists for the "hide this finding" concept; keeping them cleanly separate.

Optional note captured via `window.prompt` on override; audit trail (`overridden by X on Y — "note"`) surfaces in the tooltip on the "Sev override: ..." chip.

Gated by `getCurrentChapterUserOrLegacy()`; `chapter_staff` + `agency_operator` + `client_employee` all allowed to override findings they can see (RLS enforcement at middleware; explicit role check is defensive). Attribution captured via `user.email`.
  - **Effective severity** (`severity_override ?? severity_weight`) respected everywhere: rail color at the left of each card, sort order in the filtered list, `sevClass` styling. When overridden, an italicized `Sev override: X (rule said Y)` chip surfaces both values inline so nothing's hidden.

### Recommendations dedup Part 2 — write-time dedup + severity-band bucketing (July 12, 2026 late-late)
- **Full engine rewrite** per the spec Claude Chat produced (see the Part 2 spec doc in the July 12 session log). Executed against the actual repo with 3 clarifications resolved during handoff (uuid vs bigint on `prior_finding_id`, `dedup_ordinal` column added to freeze escalation semantics against bucket-function drift, UI update bundled in same PR).
- **Design pillars locked:**
  1. **Write-time dedup, not compare-time.** One row per active `(client_key, rule_id, subject_key)`, updated in place while active. When the rule stops firing, sweep transitions the row to `state='resolved'`.
  2. **4 states preserved** (`new` / `standing` / `changed` / `resolved`). No fifth state added — attenuation captured via `confidence` transitions on the row, not via state.
  3. **Substance change = severity band shift, escalation-asymmetric.** Escalation to a more severe band triggers `state='changed'`. De-escalation while still triggering stays `standing` (attenuation visible via confidence tier).
  4. **Default coarse quartile bucketing** for unimplemented rules — the first numeric field in a conventional order (`pct_change` / `bot_share` / `stitching_share` / `current_share_pct` / `score`) buckets into `q1-0-25` / `q2-25-50` / `q3-50-100` / `q4-100-plus`. Rules with compound or trend-shape metrics override via `dedup_bucket` + `severity_ordinal` on their `RuleEvaluationResult`.
  5. **Resolved-lookup bug fixed inherently.** `lookupActiveFinding` explicitly filters `state != 'resolved'`; `lookupPriorResolvedFinding` is the only path that reads resolved rows, and it's explicit about that.
- **Schema (all additive):** [migration `recommendations_part2_schema`](chapter-scripts/snapshots/) adds `first_observed_at timestamptz NOT NULL`, `last_observed_at timestamptz NOT NULL`, `run_count integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1`, `prior_finding_id uuid REFERENCES findings(id)`, `dedup_bucket jsonb`, `dedup_ordinal smallint`, `history jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'`. Two new partial indexes: `idx_findings_active_lookup` on active rows for the write-time dedup lookup; `idx_findings_resolved_lookup` on resolved rows for the prior-finding lookup.
- **Per-rule severity bands (declared in evaluator source alongside the rule for inspectability):**
  - **R1.1** — `elevated-30-50` (ordinal 1) / `elevated-50-70` (ordinal 2) / `elevated-70-plus` (ordinal 3). EOS at 62% bot_share sits in `elevated-50-70` — same band 4 weeks running → `state='standing'` (was `changed`×3 pre-Part-2).
  - **R4.1** — `growth-20-50` (ord 1) / `growth-50-100` (ord 2) / `growth-100-plus` (ord 3). Bucketed on `pct_change`.
  - **R4.2** — `time-30-70` (ord 1) / `time-70-100` (ord 2) / `time-100-plus` (ord 3). Bucketed on `pct_change`.
- **Engine rewrite** in [refresh-recommendations/route.ts](src/app/api/internal/cron/refresh-recommendations/route.ts): replaced `determineState` + inline INSERT with `writeFindingRow` + `lookupActiveFinding` + `lookupPriorResolvedFinding` + `defaultBucket`. Update path: comparing `bucket.ordinal > active.dedup_ordinal` decides escalation; equal buckets → `standing`; lower ordinal → `standing` (attenuation). Prior observation snapshot appended to `history` (capped at last 20 entries). Insert path: sets `prior_finding_id` from most recent resolved row if any. `markStaleAsResolved` now uses `last_observed_at < run_started_at` filter — any active row not touched this run → `resolved`.
- **Migration on EOS**: collapsed 12 non-resolved rows → 4 canonical rows. R1.1 / R4.1 / R4.2 each collapsed 4 weekly rows → 1 canonical with `run_count=4`, `state='standing'`, `history_len=3` (3 prior observations preserved with confidence + raw_metrics + state_transition_to per snapshot). R1.3 bug repair: the `state='changed'` row that should have been `state='new'` was rewritten with `prior_finding_id` pointing at its most recent resolved row. Backfill on `dedup_bucket` + `dedup_ordinal` populated via SQL that mirrors the TS bucket functions.
- **UI updates** in [RecommendationsClient.tsx](src/app/chapter/(authed)/recommendations/RecommendationsClient.tsx): dropped Part 1's client-side `collapsedCurrent` groupBy — engine now writes the collapsed shape at the DB layer, so client-side aggregation is dead code. `Nw running` chip reads `run_count` directly from the row instead of counting collapsed group size. Hero counters (Active / Strong / New) read directly from `current` without needing collapse. `dashboard-rpc.ts` SELECT extended to include the 4 new display-relevant columns (`first_observed_at` / `last_observed_at` / `run_count` / `prior_finding_id`). Current-view sort switched from `generated_at DESC` to `last_observed_at DESC` so recently-observed findings surface first.
- **Deferred UI enhancements** (columns exist in DB, wire when needed):
  - **"Seen before · resolved Xw ago" chip** driven by `prior_finding_id` — column populated on re-fires; UI can consume when we want it
  - **History-array expansion view** on row click — `history` jsonb holds the trajectory; renderer can show prior confidence tiers, prior raw_metrics, prior state transitions
  - **`first_observed_at` caption** ("since June 15") next to the running-weeks chip
- **Rejected alternative documented in the spec:** compare-time dedup (bucket at comparison, insert weekly regardless) — kept append-only but didn't attack row growth. Rejected because findings is a state machine, not a metric warehouse; time-series charts of metrics belong on source tables the evaluators read from. Rationale preserved in-file so future revisits have context.
- **Testing status:** engine passed TypeScript compile; DB state verified after migration + backfill; next Monday's 06:00 UTC cron will be the first live run against the new logic. Expected observations: R1.1/R4.1/R4.2 will produce `state='standing'` (same band as current row); R1.3 will produce whatever its evaluator computes (currently no evaluator wired — R1.3 was seeded manually pre-Part-2). If a rule's metrics escalate to a higher ordinal band, `state='changed'` will emit correctly.

### Recommendations dedup Part 1 — UI-side collapse of repeated week-over-week findings (July 12, 2026 late)
- **Fix for the "R1.1 duplicated for 4 weeks" complaint.** Investigation confirmed the state machine (`determineState` in [refresh-recommendations/route.ts:250](src/app/api/internal/cron/refresh-recommendations/route.ts#L250)) does byte-exact `JSON.stringify` comparison of `raw_metrics` between the new and prior findings. For rules whose metrics are moving averages (R1.1 `bot_share`, R4.1 median path length, R4.2 median time-to-close), the value drifts every week → flagged `changed` → new row inserted → UI showed 4 cards for the same finding. **Zero `standing` rows exist anywhere in the DB across any rule** — the state machine is essentially always producing `changed` because tiny drift is treated as a real change.
- **Part 1 fix (this ship): UI-side collapse.** In [RecommendationsClient.tsx](src/app/chapter/(authed)/recommendations/RecommendationsClient.tsx), the Current view now groups findings by `(rule_id, subject_key ?? "")`, sorts each group by `generated_at DESC`, and keeps only the latest — attaching `weeksRunning` (group count) + `firstSeenAt` (earliest row) to the returned display object. Card renders a small orange "Nw running" chip when `weeksRunning > 1` with a tooltip showing first-observed date. History view still passes raw rows (chronological log by design). Hero counters (Active / Strong signal / New this week) run off the collapsed set — so 4 R1.1 rows count as 1 recommendation, not 4.
- **Effect on EOS today:** dashboard collapses from 12 raw rows across 4 rules → **4 cards** — R1.1 (62% bot / 4w running), R4.1 (12 touchpoints / 4w running), R4.2 (17 days to close / 4w running), R1.3 (38% identity stitch / 1w). Chips make it obvious at a glance which findings are new vs longstanding.
- **Deliberately NOT in Part 1:** trend indicator (e.g. `60% → 62%` sparkline). Would require per-rule primary-metric declaration; skipped for v1. Rule copy already conveys the movement in the headline text where relevant.
- **Part 2 queued (Claude Chat spec first):** evaluator-side dedup normalization. Each rule declares a `dedup_bucket` alongside `raw_metrics` — coarsened values used only for `determineState` comparison (e.g. `bot_share_bucket: "50-70%"` instead of exact 62). Small drift → `standing`, real shift → `changed`. Reduces DB row growth going forward. Requires touching every evaluator + per-rule bucketing spec.
- **Also queued as Claude Chat specs:** (a) Recommendations outcomes verification — baseline snapshot at findings-emission + follow-up measurement + attribution model for whether a recommendation actually worked; (b) tab-by-tab dashboard info + effectiveness review — persona checklist + per-tab audit of what a first-time operator understands vs guesses vs misinterprets.

### Practice + sales-demo playground shipped at `/demo` (July 12, 2026 late)
- **Rebuild of the existing `/demo` page** (previously a faux-news iframe + pixel event stream) into a proper Moment Identity v2 playground. Two use cases in one surface: (a) operator practice — author a prompt at `/internal/identity-prompts/adsforgood_prod`, then come here to see it fire against a real DOM without embedding on a real client's storefront; (b) sales demo — walk a prospect through each preset with clickable triggers + a live event feed showing what the pixel captures in real time.
- **File:** [src/app/demo/page.tsx](src/app/demo/page.tsx) — client component, 6-card grid (one per MI v2 preset) + session-stat strip + live event feed at bottom (2s auto-refresh, reuses existing `/demo/snapshot` route). Header links to `/internal/identity-prompts/adsforgood_prod` for prompt config, `/offers` for review queue, `/offer-thresholds` for auto-accept config, `/templates` for email authoring.
- **5 shipped presets seeded in DB** under `adsforgood_prod` tenant so the demo works out-of-the-box on first visit:
  - `demo_email_exchange` — Email Exchange (v1.5 shape), click_element `.demo-email-exchange`, offer code `DEMO10`
  - `demo_custom_form` — Custom Form with 3 fields (email + role single-choice + notes textarea), click_element `.demo-custom-form`
  - `demo_notification` — Custom Notification, bubble container, position bottom-right, yes/no CTA, click_element `.demo-notification`
  - `demo_make_offer` — Make an Offer, target = mock hoodie ($89.00), click_element `.demo-make-offer`
  - `demo_phone_call` — Phone Call with 2 CTAs (sales + support), click_element `.demo-phone-call`
- **All 5 use `frequency=every_visit`** — no session/visitor throttle so operators can click repeatedly during practice/demo without dismissal-persistence getting in the way. Real production prompts should still use `session` or `visitor` frequency; the demo variant is deliberate.
- **Pixel auto-loads via existing ChapterLoader** (unchanged) — `data-client-key="adsforgood_prod"`, `data-vertical="AFG_SITE"`. On page load, pixel calls `/api/chapter/identity-prompts?client_key=adsforgood_prod`, gets the 5 seeded prompts, registers click-element listeners for each. Visitor clicks a trigger → matching prompt fires.
- **Remind Me tile visible but disabled** with "Phase 6 · not shipped" badge. Placeholder note explains what Phase 6 will bring (subscription monitor + hourly evaluation cron + inventory/price polling adapter). Operator can't accidentally try to configure it.
- **`configHints` on each card documents the exact form config** (slug + trigger type + CSS selector + preset-specific fields) so operators editing at `/internal/identity-prompts/adsforgood_prod` don't have to guess. Copy-pastable for adapting the seeded demos into new prompts.
- **Make-an-Offer demo end-to-end without Shopify:** when `PLATFORM_ADAPTER_MODE=mock` env var is set, shopify-mock returns realistic `CHAPTER-XXXXXXXX` codes without hitting a real Shopify store. Live for Shopify sales conversations without needing a paid Shopify Partners dev store.
- **`/faux-news` route left in place** — orphaned (nothing links to it after this rebuild) but not blocking. Delete in a future cleanup pass if the URL is truly unused; low-priority since it's under 100 lines and harmless.
- **`/demo/snapshot` route unchanged** — still queries recent pixel_events for the given client_key. Now consumed only by the new playground, but the endpoint contract is untouched so any external integration would still work.

### NSC historical ad-signal backfill — 9 Google Ads visitors stitched retroactively (July 12, 2026)
- **Companion to the July 10 cross-subdomain identity fix.** Yesterday's fix stops the fragmentation forward; this backfill recovers a slice of historical bookings that were fragmented before the fix landed. Only tackles the ad-signal-carrying page_views — the highest-value slice for the Shopify sales narrative and the tightest match-confidence.
- **Match logic (DB-only, no code):** for each pixel event on notsocavalier.com in the last 30 days carrying `gclid` / `gbraid` / `wbraid` / `fbclid` or `utm_medium IN ('cpc','paid','ppc')`, find a Book Now `redirect_click` where (a) same city + region on both sides (both non-null), (b) `book_ts > page_ts`, (c) `book_ts - page_ts <= 60 min`, (d) different `identity_key` (the fragmentation signature). 1:1 matching via `ROW_NUMBER()` — each Book Now click pairs with its nearest-in-time page_view; each page_view visitor keeps its strongest signal match.
- **Yield: 9 pairs matched.** All 9 are Google Ads (`gclid`), all Philadelphia metro (Bensalem NJ, Blackwood NJ, Malvern PA×2, Norristown PA, Philadelphia PA, Springfield PA, Upper Darby PA, Villanova PA), gap between page_view and Book Now click 0–8 seconds (average 6 seconds — classic "click ad → land → click Book Now" flow). Very low false-positive risk given the tight time window in geographically-tagged metros.
- **Insert shape:** `identity_aliases (client_key='not_so_cavalier', from_identity_key=page_anon, to_identity_key=book_anon, method='probabilistic_geo_time_ad_signal', confidence=70, is_deterministic=false, reason='historical_ad_stitch_backfill', metadata={ad_type, gclid, city, region, page_ts, book_ts, gap_minutes, match_criteria, backfill_run_ts})`. `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` on the alias unique constraint so re-runs are idempotent.
- **Canonical trigger walked the graph correctly on all 9.** Post-insert canonical states:
  - **6 of 9 unified to `email_sha256:X`** (best case — Book Now was previously stitched to a booking's email via Sprint 4 probabilistic time-window matcher; the ad-attributed page_view now shares that same email canonical, closing the ad → booking chain end-to-end)
  - **3 of 9 unified to shared `anonymous_id:<uuid>`** (Bensalem, Blackwood, Malvern one of two — Book Now click didn't stitch to a booking, so no email attached; the page_view and Book Now are correctly linked as one visitor but no booking to attribute yet)
- **Downstream effect:** tonight's 03:30 UTC attribution chain refresh will pick up the 6 email-linked canonicals. `canonical_v1`'s session-entry classifier will see the earliest event (the gclid-carrying page_view) and classify the chapter as **Paid Search (Google Ads)** — where before it was `(direct)` fallback in canonical_v2. First 6 NSC bookings ever with real Google Ads attribution.
- **Rejected relaxations** (documented in case they surface later): loosening to null-null geo matches (~30% of pool has both nulls; too coarse to distinguish visitors); extending window to 90 min (marginal gain, higher noise); accepting utm-only paid signals across-city (too loose without a shared IP or UA hash to tighten). Kept the tight criteria to preserve trust in the alias graph — 9 high-confidence recoveries beats 30 mixed-confidence ones for the sales narrative.
- **Nothing to backfill for the non-ad-signal slice.** The general population of fragmented visitors (visitors who came from organic search, direct, or organic social and got fragmented) would require geo + device + time + no-signal matching, which is much noisier. Skipped by design — the forward fix (yesterday) makes that gap self-resolve for new visitors, and retroactively unifying anonymous browsing sessions with unknown source doesn't buy much analytical value.
- **How to run again if operator wants to sweep the fbclid + utm-paid slices** or extend the window: the exact query is in the Supabase execute_sql history for July 12; can be adapted with different WHERE predicates. Idempotent on re-run via ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING.

### Cross-subdomain identity unification SHIPPED — pixel + redirect handler now share cookies (July 10, 2026)
- **Load-bearing correctness bug closed.** The pixel API at `chapter.<client>.com/api/pixel/collect` writes cookies `up_anon_${client_key}` + `up_journey_${client_key}` on the `.notsocavalier.com` apex. The redirect handler on the same origin at `/r/<client>/<slug>` was reading + writing DIFFERENT cookie names (`chapter_identity` + `chapter_journey`). Comment in [identity.ts](src/app/lib/redirect/identity.ts) literally said "Same cookie names that pixel.js writes" — but that was aspirational, never actually true.
- **Diagnostic:** Zero overlap observed at NSC in the last 24h — **60 distinct page_view anonymous_ids from notsocavalier.com** vs **45 distinct redirect_click anonymous_ids from chapter.notsocavalier.com**, share count = **0**. Every visitor who landed via a redirect + also browsed the storefront pixel was minting two separate identities and never linking them. Explains the July 10 morning finding that all 12 Book Now stitched bookings had zero pre-click page_views on the same anonymous_id — same visitor, two identities.
- **Yesterday's fragmentation fix (July 9) fixed the FORMAT and APEX** (raw uuid → `anonymous_id:<uuid>` prefix, cookies scoped to `.notsocavalier.com` instead of host-only). But it left the NAME mismatch untouched. Cookies were correctly scoped and correctly formatted, they just weren't the same cookies.
- **The fix (3 files):**
  - **[src/app/lib/redirect/identity.ts](src/app/lib/redirect/identity.ts)** — `resolveIdentity(req, clientKey)` + `applyIdentityCookies(res, resolved, hostname, clientKey)` now take `clientKey` and read/write `up_anon_${client_key}` + `up_journey_${client_key}`. Cookie stores **raw UUID** (36 chars) matching the pixel API's `UUID_REGEX.test(existingAnon)` validation gate — pixel API rejects prefixed values as invalid so we had to strip the prefix at the cookie layer. Downstream code that needs the prefixed identity key gets it via a new `rawAnonymousId` field on `ResolvedIdentity` (cookie value) alongside `identityKey` (prefixed, used for click logs + alias inserts). `SameSite=None; Secure` (was `Lax`) — required for the pixel's cross-origin fetch from notsocavalier.com → chapter.notsocavalier.com to send the cookie back. Journey TTL bumped from 1 day → 30 days to match pixel API's convention.
  - **[src/app/api/chapter/pixel.js/route.ts](src/app/api/chapter/pixel.js/route.ts)** — new `getOrCreateIdWithCookieFallback(storageKey, cookieName)` helper. Priority: localStorage first → cookie fallback → mint new. Applied at all three sites where the pixel resolves anonymous_id / journey_id (initial cache setup + inner `send()` rehydration + Tier 1 redirect `?chid=` handoff site). When a visitor lands on `notsocavalier.com` via a redirect (which set `up_anon_not_so_cavalier=X` on `.notsocavalier.com`), the pixel's localStorage on `notsocavalier.com` origin is empty → cookie fallback finds X → uses X + syncs to localStorage → all subsequent pixel events also use X. One identity across the whole visit.
  - **[src/app/r/[client_key]/[slug]/route.ts](src/app/r/[client_key]/[slug]/route.ts)** — pass `client_key` to `resolveIdentity` + `applyIdentityCookies`. One-line calls each.
- **Historical fragmentation unrecoverable.** Past visitors whose identity split across the two cookies can't be linked retroactively — their anonymous_id pairs aren't tied together by any signal that survived. Going forward: unified from the moment the fix deploys.
- **What this unlocks (forward-only):** ad-attribution chain finally reconnects. Future flow: Google Ad click → `chapter.notsocavalier.com/r/not_so_cavalier/google-ads?to=notsocavalier.com&gclid=...` sets `up_anon_not_so_cavalier=X` on `.notsocavalier.com` → 302 to notsocavalier.com → pixel reads X from cookie fallback → page_views tagged with X + captures `gclid` → visitor clicks Book Now → redirect handler reads X from its cookie → click stitches to X → booking webhook lands, stitcher matches booking canonical to X → canonical_v1 session-entry classifier sees gclid on the earliest pixel event → attribution routes to Paid Search. Same chain works for organic search (referrer captured on landing page_view), organic social (referrer), email (utm_source=email), and direct.
- **Backward-compat trade-off (accepted):** existing visitors holding old `chapter_identity=anonymous_id:X` cookies will get a fresh anonymous_id on their next redirect click (one-time regression for those visitors). Not worth writing a grace-period reader — the old cookie was already causing fragmentation, so continuing to read it wouldn't help future events on notsocavalier.com anyway. Old cookies will expire on their 365-day TTL from whenever they were last touched.
- **Deploy verification checklist** (post-deploy):
  1. Curl `chapter.notsocavalier.com/r/not_so_cavalier/book-now?to=https://book.squareup.com/...` — inspect `Set-Cookie` in response, confirm `up_anon_not_so_cavalier=<uuid>` and `up_journey_not_so_cavalier=<uuid>` with `Domain=.notsocavalier.com; SameSite=None; Secure`
  2. Wait 24 hours + re-run the overlap query — expect share count > 0 (visitors who both hit the pixel AND the redirect within 24h)
  3. Watch new bookings in `chapter_ingest.purchase_events` — expect at least some stitched bookings' canonical graphs to include utm/partner_ids on the earliest pixel event (proof the chain is reconnecting)
- **Lovable-side companion items still queued** (see July 10 conversation): `?src=<page_key>` param on Book Now buttons to backup Referrer-Policy stripping; `<meta name="referrer" content="origin">` so cross-subdomain Referer at least sends the origin. Both nice-to-have, not blocking now that the identity layer is fixed.

### MI v2 Phase 5 — Make an Offer SHIPPED end-to-end (July 9, 2026)
- **Executes the Path A plan** locked July 5 (see next entry). One session ships all 8 sub-phases: platform adapter layer, offers logic, submit endpoint, admin review queue, threshold config UI, pixel renderer. Clean TypeScript compile throughout. Mocked-adapter dev pattern demoable without a live Shopify store.
- **Phase 5.1 — Platform adapter types.** [src/app/lib/platform/types.ts](src/app/lib/platform/types.ts): `PlatformAdapter` interface with `name`, `isSupported(feature)`, `getProduct(client_key, product_id)`, `createDiscountCode(client_key, config)`. `PlatformFeature` union: `"discount_code" | "cart_detection" | "inventory_polling" | "price_polling"`. `ProductInfo`, `DiscountCodeConfig`, `DiscountCode` shapes. Adapters are stateless singletons; caller picks one via `selectAdapter(client_key)` and reuses per request.
- **Phase 5.2 — Adapter implementations + selector.**
  - [shopify.ts](src/app/lib/platform/shopify.ts) — real Shopify Admin GraphQL. `readShopifyCreds(esp_credentials_jsonb)` extracts `shopify_access_token` + `shop_domain`; `getProduct` + `createDiscountCode` currently stubbed with implementation notes (priceRuleCreate + priceRuleDiscountCodeCreate mutations). Post-signing: fill in the two GraphQL calls, adapter goes live automatically.
  - [shopify-mock.ts](src/app/lib/platform/shopify-mock.ts) — MOCK_PRODUCTS registry with 3 realistic fake products (hoodie / tee / jacket). `generateCode(prefix)` mints codes like `CHAPTER-9F3XK2QR` using randomBytes + confusable-char-free alphabet. Synthesizes product data for unknown IDs. 250ms artificial latency for realism. Returned data matches Shopify's real shape so downstream code paths (state machine, email delivery, admin UI) can't tell mock from real.
  - [square.ts](src/app/lib/platform/square.ts), [custom.ts](src/app/lib/platform/custom.ts) — stubs. Square returns null (Catalog API integration deferred); custom generates code strings only (operator manually honors).
  - [selector.ts](src/app/lib/platform/selector.ts) — `selectAdapter(client_key)` picks adapter based on `client.platform` + `esp_credentials_jsonb` + `PLATFORM_ADAPTER_MODE` env var. Selection matrix: `shopify` + creds + real mode → shopifyReal; `shopify` + no creds → shopifyMock (fallback); `shopify` + mode=mock → forced shopifyMock; `square` → squareAdapter; `custom` → customAdapter. 5-min in-memory cache on client config lookups.
- **Phase 5.3 — Offers logic.**
  - [types.ts](src/app/lib/offers/types.ts) — `OfferStatus` (7 values matching CHECK constraint on `chapter_engagement.offers.status`); `OfferTargetResource` discriminated union (product / collection / cart / storewide); `OfferDecision` discriminated union with 4 actions (auto_accept / counter / decline / review).
  - [state-machine.ts](src/app/lib/offers/state-machine.ts) — `VALID_TRANSITIONS` DAG. `pending_review` → {manually_accepted, countered, declined, expired}. `countered` → {manually_accepted, declined, expired}. `auto_accepted` + `manually_accepted` → `redeemed`. Terminals: declined, expired, redeemed. **No CHECK constraint enforces transitions server-side** — this module IS the enforcement (intentional so backfill / repair operations don't need special privilege).
  - [evaluator.ts](src/app/lib/offers/evaluator.ts) — deterministic pure function. Threshold hierarchy: product-specific → collection → global → hardcoded fallback (90% of list_price). Both threshold_pct + threshold_absolute can be set on a row; the stricter (higher) wins. Counter zone: bid between COUNTER_FLOOR_PCT (60%) and threshold → offer halfway between bid and threshold. Below floor → decline. No threshold + no list_price → route to manual review. 60s in-memory cache on threshold row loads with `clearThresholdCache(client_key)` bust exposed for admin actions.
- **Phase 5.4 — Submit endpoint.** [POST /api/chapter/offer-submit](src/app/api/chapter/offer-submit/route.ts) mirrors the `/api/chapter/prompt-response` defense pattern: honeypot (`hp_field`) + HMAC session token (`verifyPromptSession` — 30-min TTL, minted by `/api/chapter/identity-prompts` GET) + per-IP rate limit (**10/hr — stricter than prompt-response's 20/hr** because every auto-accepted offer creates a real discount code + outbound email, so abuse cost is higher). Every rejection logs via `logAuthAttempt` so the attack-alert cron picks them up. Validates prompt belongs to client + enabled + `preset_type='make_an_offer'` before insert. Auto-accept path: fetch product → evaluate → insert offer row (status=`auto_accepted`) → mint discount code via adapter → save code → fire-and-forget `welcome_offer` email. Counter path: insert row (status=`countered`) → send `offer_counter` email. Decline path: insert with reason. Review path: insert (status=`pending_review`), operator handles via admin UI. Returns `{ offer_id, decision, counter_amount? }`.
- **Phase 5.5 — Admin review queue at [/internal/identity-prompts/[clientKey]/offers](src/app/internal/identity-prompts/[clientKey]/offers/page.tsx).**
  - Server page reads `chapter_engagement.offers` for the client, filterable by status via `?status=` searchParam. Status pill filter chips with per-status counts across the top.
  - `truncateIdentity(key)` renders as `email_sha256:abc12345…` — 8-char hash prefix, same privacy pattern as other operator surfaces.
  - `summarizeTarget(t)` describes the target resource in English (product name + list price / collection name / cart snapshot / storewide).
  - [OffersList.tsx](src/app/internal/identity-prompts/[clientKey]/offers/OffersList.tsx) client component: status-color chip family + inline Approve / Counter / Decline buttons on `pending_review` rows. Optimistic UI with error-revert; alert() on failures.
  - [_actions.ts](src/app/internal/identity-prompts/[clientKey]/offers/_actions.ts) server actions: `approveOffer` mints code via adapter + updates offer row + fires welcome_offer email; `counterOffer` prompts operator for amount + fires offer_counter email; `declineOffer` transitions to declined. All 3 gated by `chapter_staff` role via `getCurrentChapterUserOrLegacy()`. Transitions gated by `isValidTransition(current_status, target_status)` from the state machine.
  - **Deferred: `resolveRecipientEmail(identity_key)`** — currently returns null so emails silently no-op on the operator-transition path. Offer row still lands + code is generated + admin UI reflects state; only the outbound send is pending. Not blocking for demo purposes because the submit endpoint's auto-accept path DOES have the recipient email (it comes in the request body). Follow-up: resolve email from either `chapter_config.email_engagement_events.recipient_token` or a per-offer stored contact field.
- **Phase 5.6 — Threshold config UI at [/internal/identity-prompts/[clientKey]/offer-thresholds](src/app/internal/identity-prompts/[clientKey]/offer-thresholds/page.tsx).** Structured form (scope picker: product / collection / global; target_id required for non-global; threshold_pct 0-100 OR threshold_absolute $ float; internal notes). List rows sorted by hierarchy rank (product → collection → global). RowActions component supports Deactivate / Reactivate (sets `active=false` — never hard-deletes, audit trail sticks). Server actions gated by `chapter_staff` role. Every create/update busts `clearThresholdCache(client_key)` so next bid evaluation sees fresh config.
- **Phase 5.7 — Pixel renderer.** `chapterRenderPromptMakeAnOffer(prompt)` added to [pixel.js/route.ts](src/app/api/chapter/pixel.js/route.ts). Dispatch: `preset_type='make_an_offer'` routes here. Modal with content blocks (headline + body) + optional product summary + bid input + email input + honeypot + submit. Client-side email hash → `api.identify(email_sha256:...)` fires before POST so downstream events stitch immediately. Then `chapterPostOfferSubmit` POSTs to `/api/chapter/offer-submit`. Success state swaps to decision-appropriate copy: auto_accept → "Offer accepted, code emailed"; counter → "Counter-offer sent at $X, check your inbox"; decline → gentle rejection; review → "we'll follow up." Fires `identity_prompt_shown` on render + `identity_prompt_submitted` on decision with the `decision` field on the payload so operators can measure show → submit → decision funnel.
- **Discoverability:** per-client prompts page now shows "Offers →" + "Offer thresholds →" nav links alongside existing "Email templates →" + "View responses →" chips.
- **What Phase 5.5 explicitly deferred but doesn't block the sales demo:**
  - Real outbound email on operator-transition paths (auto_accept path IS wired since recipient_email lives in the request body; admin-triggered approve/counter emails silently no-op until recipient resolution ships)
  - `redeemed` state hook — no post-checkout webhook wired yet to detect code redemption. State machine has the transition slot; just no producer.
  - Bulk operator actions (approve N pending offers at once); scan of expired offers to auto-transition
- **What Phase 5.7 explicitly deferred:**
  - ~~Preset-specific admin form builder~~ — SHIPPED same-day follow-up (July 9). [MakeAnOfferBuilder.tsx](src/app/internal/identity-prompts/[clientKey]/MakeAnOfferBuilder.tsx) captures content blocks (headline + body) + target picker (product / collection / storewide) with per-type inputs (product_id + product_name + list_price for product; collection_id + collection_name for collection). Wired into `PromptForm.tsx` as a full `make_an_offer` branch mirroring the notification/phone_call pattern (slug + trigger + submit-button label + builder + frequency + enabled). Picker tile un-greyed. `PromptFormInput.container_jsonb` widened to allow the `target` field. `validate` in `_actions.ts` relaxed to skip the `headline required` check for composable presets (headlines live in `content_blocks_jsonb` instead of the top-level column). Now demoable end-to-end without hand-authoring JSON.
  - Multi-product bid modal (visitor picks from a list of eligible products in-modal); today it's one target per prompt
  - Progressive disclosure of counter amount (currently prompt is a native `window.prompt`; a nicer inline picker is polish)

### MI v2 Phase 5+6 planning — Path A locked (Shopify-focused, adapter-ready structure) (July 5, 2026 evening, no code yet)
- **Trigger for planning:** operator has a Shopify store owner meeting next week; wants to demo Chapter's Make an Offer + Remind Me features before committing engineering time on a specific client integration.
- **Refresher on the two phases:**
  - **Phase 5 — Make an Offer.** Visitor puts an item in cart, hits abandonment trigger, sees modal: "Would you like this for $X?" Backend logic auto-accepts / counter-offers / declines based on `chapter_config.offer_thresholds` (product / collection / global default hierarchy). On auto-accept: generate a unique Shopify discount code via priceRules API, deliver via Resend email. Manual review queue for edge cases at `/internal/identity-prompts/<key>/offers`. State machine on `chapter_engagement.offers` (pending_review / auto_accepted / manually_accepted / countered / declined / expired / redeemed).
  - **Phase 6 — Remind Me.** Visitor viewing product → "Notify me when back in stock" or "when price drops." Creates row in `chapter_engagement.subscriptions`. Hourly cron at `/api/internal/cron/evaluate-subscriptions` polls Shopify inventory + price → fires email notifications on trigger. Auto-cancel subscription on same-visitor purchase (`/api/purchase` hook).
- **Path A locked (Shopify-focused, adapter-ready structure).** Builds core (universal UI + state machines + tables + cron + email delivery) + full Shopify adapter in `src/app/lib/platform/shopify.ts`. Leaves `square.ts` + `custom.ts` as `null`-returning stubs. Add real implementations for those platforms when specific clients ask. ~1 week focused effort for both phases end-to-end. **NOT Path B** (full platform-agnostic build at ~1.5-2 weeks) — non-Shopify demand doesn't exist yet.
- **Mocked-adapter dev pattern:** demo build can proceed WITHOUT a live Shopify store. Two adapter files:
  - `shopify.ts` — real Shopify Admin API calls; gated on `chapter_config.clients.esp_credentials_jsonb` credential presence
  - `shopify-mock.ts` — returns fake data (`CHAPTER-ABC12345` codes, static price/inventory) for build + demo
  Adapter selection via env var. Full UI, state machine, email delivery, admin dashboard all real. Only the outbound API call to Shopify is mocked. On client-signing, swap credentials in DB → adapter transparently switches to real Shopify calls. **Zero code changes needed post-signing.**
- **What the demo shows without a real store:**
  - Visitor experience: modal, bid input, confirmation (real UI, real behavior)
  - Backend: threshold logic runs, decision made, code generated (mocked value, realistic format)
  - Email delivery: real Resend send, real inbox delivery, real code visible in email
  - Admin experience: `/internal/identity-prompts/<key>/offers` with real state machine + review queue
  - Analytics: real `chapter_engagement.offers` rows + `identity_prompt_submitted` pixel events
- **Optional realism boost for demo day:** free Shopify Partners dev store (~30 min setup) lets you show one code being generated + redeemed on a real (test) Shopify store during the demo — "we know this actually works" proof point. Not required.
- **Existing foundation to build on (all shipped in prior MI v2 phases):**
  - `chapter_engagement` schema + `subscriptions` + `offers` tables (already created in Phase 1)
  - `chapter_config.offer_thresholds` table (already created)
  - `chapter_config.clients.platform text DEFAULT 'custom'` column + `esp_credentials_jsonb` column
  - Adapter shells in `src/app/lib/platform/{shopify,square,custom}.ts` (skeleton only)
  - Email send infrastructure (Phase 3): Resend adapter + template composition
  - Admin UI patterns from Phase 4 (Custom Notification + Phone Call presets)
- **Next-session actionable when picked up:** ship Phase 5 first (Make an Offer) — user's sales conversation next week is Shopify-focused, and Phase 5 is the more differentiated feature. Phase 6 (Remind Me) can follow depending on time + demand.

### NSC Book Now wrap shipped on Lovable + Category B RPCs Tier 1 + Tier 2 all switched to chapter_purchase_summary (July 5, 2026 evening)
- **NSC Book Now Tier 1 redirect wrap SHIPPED on Lovable.** Operator find-and-replaced all 20 instances of the raw Square booking URL (`https://book.squareup.com/appointments/lfi1vnwnd57jf7/location/L9Z08X3BZXQJB/services`) with the Chapter wrap (`https://chapter.notsocavalier.com/r/not_so_cavalier/book-now?to=https://book.squareup.com/appointments/lfi1vnwnd57jf7/location/L9Z08X3BZXQJB/services`) across 6 files (Navbar.tsx, About.tsx, Contact.tsx, Index.tsx, Services.tsx, Team.tsx). The old URL was a full absolute URL so it's a safe substring inside the new URL — one clean find-and-replace pass, no risk of over-matching. Also swapped the URL inside `window.gtag_report_booking(...)` calls to keep Google Ads conversion tracking parameter in sync with the href.
- **Google Ads tracking template + Book Now wrap don't interfere.** NSC's Google Ads account-level template already wraps ad clicks under a DIFFERENT slug (`/r/not_so_cavalier/google-ads`). Book Now is `/r/not_so_cavalier/book-now`. Two separate redirect rules, no collision. A visitor's typical journey now: Google ad click → Chapter's google-ads redirect (logs `redirect_click` #1 with gclid + utm) → landing on notsocavalier.com (pixel builds session) → clicks Book Now → Chapter's book-now redirect (logs `redirect_click` #2, gtag_report_booking fires "Booking initiated" in Google Ads) → Square booking → Square webhook → identity_canon merge → tonight's attribution chain refresh picks it up. All Chapter cookies (`.notsocavalier.com` scoped) persist across both redirects.
- **Test click verification:** 3 test clicks landed cleanly in `chapter_ingest.pixel_events`:
  - anonymous_id consistent across all 3 clicks (same browser session)
  - journey_id consistent
  - `props->>'destination'` includes `book.squareup.com/appointments/...?chid=anonymous_id%3A...&jid=...` (chid/jid handoff params appended by Sprint 4's `appendIdentityHandoff`)
  - `props->'full_query'->>'to'` = raw Square URL (matches stitcher's ILIKE filter)
  - `matched_rule_id: null` (correct — fallback path via `?to=`, no rule needed; hit_count analytics on a rule is the only reason to add one)
  - device_type: desktop, country: US, all geo/device metadata captured
- **First real booking through the wrapped flow will validate the stitcher's alias insertion** — anonymous_id from the redirect_click → aliased to square_customer_id (from Square webhook) → canon graph propagates to email_sha256 canonical. Deferred check for natural verification over next few days.
- **8 Category B RPCs (5 Tier 1 + 3 Tier 2) all switched from `chapter_channel_paths_canonical_v1_snapshot + boundary_value` → `chapter_purchase_summary + canonical_v2_snapshot`.** All commits from today: `e63e24c` (Tier 1) + `ca2babb` (Tier 2).
- **Tier 1 (highest-value NSC dashboard tiles):**
  - `channel_performance_overview`: promoted the v2 preview to primary. Reads chapters from `chapter_purchase_summary` (canonical_v2 path + payment-attributed net_revenue). EOS: (unknown) $1,502 → (direct) via canonical_v2 fallback attribution. NSC: (unknown) $27,506 → (direct) $53,118 via payment attribution.
  - `attribution_overview`: computes first/last/linear on-the-fly from `chapter_purchase_summary`, bypasses the pre-computed `purchase_channel_final_v1` + `attribution_linear_chapter_v1` snapshots which had the same $0 boundary_value problem for NSC.
  - `path_combinations_overview` / `channel_roles_overview` / `channel_affinity_overview`: all switched to `chapter_purchase_summary` source.
- **Tier 2 (L&I page RPCs):**
  - `correlation_channel_overview`: swap converters + converting_chapters source. NSC now returns 3 rows (previously 0).
  - `contribution_overview`: swap base source, compute linear on-the-fly (bypasses `attribution_linear_chapter_v1`), use `canonical_v2_snapshot` for returning customer lookup. NSC: 1 row (direct + $53K, participation 100%, recurrence 1.0).
  - `incrementality_channel_overview`: use `canonical_v2_snapshot` for all_chapters (preserves LAG for prev_boundary_ts across all-time), LEFT JOIN `chapter_purchase_summary` for windowed net_revenue. NSC: 1 row (direct × subscriber bucket).
- **Sample dashboards deferred:** `lagged_impact_pair_series` + `matched_lift_bucket_stats` don't read `boundary_value` and stay unchanged — their identity-level math is untouched by the split-flow issue. `funnel_overview` is ecom-oriented and doesn't need modification for a barbershop's Book Now → Book chain.
- **EOS regression verification: byte-identical numbers across all 4 revenue-computing RPCs after the shift** (channel_performance / attribution first-touch / channel_roles / path_combinations). The (unknown) → (direct) shift ($1,502) is the ONLY EOS delta and is architecturally correct (canonical_v2 fallback chapters are legitimately direct-attributed, not unknown).
- **Full state of NSC dashboards after today:**
  - Category A tiles (already worked): `purchase_overview` ($27,506 orders + revenue in the last month), `journey_overview`, `dashboard_timeseries`, etc.
  - Category B tiles (fixed today): Attribution / Channels / Paths / Roles / Affinity / L&I all render real numbers, all attributing to `(direct)` today. When stitcher populates canonical_v1 session entries via redirect_click matching, revenue automatically spreads across real channels (organic search, paid search, etc.) in the next attribution chain refresh (03:30 UTC nightly). **No further RPC changes needed.**
- **On the boundary_ts vs payment_ts semantic question raised during the build:** chapter_purchase_summary attributes revenue to a chapter using the chapter's `boundary_ts` = booking's `event_ts` (when the booking record was created in Square), NOT when the appointment happened OR when payment was received. A May booking → June appointment → June payment attributes revenue to the MAY window (chapter's boundary_ts), not June. This is marketing-attribution-correct (credits the touchpoint that drove the booking commitment) but different from cash-flow accounting. Operator explicitly OK'd this — matches Chapter's boundary-event framing.

### chapter_purchase_summary RPC + Orders API integration — NSC revenue attribution 86% coverage (July 2, 2026 afternoon)
- **Problem NSC dashboards had:** canonical_v2 showed $0 revenue on every chapter because NSC's boundary event is `appointment_booked` with `value=0`. Real revenue lives in downstream `appointment_paid` events (POS transactions, split into a separate Square Order/Payment from the booking). Dashboard needed a way to attribute payment revenue back to booking chapters via JOIN.
- **Investigation exhausted:** searched every Square API surface for a deterministic booking↔payment link before falling back to a heuristic:
  - **Customer object:** no relational fields to bookings/payments/orders. `reference_id` writable but doesn't help decide which booking a specific payment paid for.
  - **Order object (payment-side):** carries `line_items[].catalog_object_id` (= booking's `service_variation_id`) + `created_by_team_member_id` (= booking's `team_member_id`). This is the signal we ended up using — deterministic for unique service+staff combinations.
